&+\frac{1}{32}\rho^{-4}Y{}^{i}{}^{j}(\gamma{}^{a}){}^{\alpha}{}^{\beta}(\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\gamma}(\gamma{}^{d}{}^{e}){}_{\gamma}{}^{\delta}\widehat F{}_{b}{}_{c}T{}_{d}{}_{e}\lambda{}_{\beta}{}_{j}\lambda{}_{\delta}{}_{i} +\frac{i}{64}\rho^{-4}Y{}^{i}{}^{j}(\gamma{}^{a}){}^{\alpha}{}^{\beta}(\gamma{}^{b}){}_{\alpha}{}_{\gamma}(\gamma{}^{c}{}^{d}){}^{\delta}{}^{\gamma}\widehat F{}_{c}{}_{d}\lambda{}_{\beta}{}_{j}\big(\mathcal D_{b}\lambda{}_{\delta}{}_{i}\big)
\end{aligned}
\tag{S6-E-754}
